 * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
 *
 * const verbose = Flag.Boolean("verbose").pipe(
 *   Flag.withFallbackConfig(Config.Boolean("VERBOSE"))
 * )
 * verbose.kind // => "flag"
 * ```
 *
 * @stability unstable
 * @category combinators
 * @since 4.0.0
 */
export declare const withFallbackConfig: {
    /**
     * Adds a fallback config that is loaded when a required flag is missing.
     *
     * **Example** (Falling back to config)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Config } from "effect"
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * const verbose = Flag.Boolean("verbose").pipe(
     *   Flag.withFallbackConfig(Config.Boolean("VERBOSE"))
     * )
     * verbose.kind // => "flag"
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category combinators
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <B>(config: Config.Config<B>): <A>(self: Flag<A>) => Flag<A | B>;
    /**
     * Adds a fallback config that is loaded when a required flag is missing.
     *
     * **Example** (Falling back to config)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Config } from "effect"
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * const verbose = Flag.Boolean("verbose").pipe(
     *   Flag.withFallbackConfig(Config.Boolean("VERBOSE"))
     * )
     * verbose.kind // => "flag"
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category combinators
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A, B>(self: Flag<A>, config: Config.Config<B>): Flag<A | B>;
};
/**
 * Adds a fallback prompt that is shown when a required flag is missing.
 *
 * **Gotchas**
 *
 * When `TerminalCapabilities.canPrompt` is `false`, the prompt is skipped and
 * the original `MissingOption` error is returned.
 *
 * **Example** (Falling back to prompts)
 *
 * ```ts import.meta.vitest
 * import { Flag, Prompt } from "effect/cli"
 *
 * const name = Flag.String("name").pipe(
 *   Flag.withFallbackPrompt(Prompt.String({ message: "Name" }))
 * )
 * name.kind // => "flag"
 * ```
 *
 * **Example** (Showing an introduction before fallback prompts)
 *
 * The prompt can be built by an effect, which only runs when the prompt is
 * needed. Share a cached introduction between the prompt builders and
 * `Command.provideEffectDiscard`: it runs once, before the first prompt, and
 * also runs when every flag is supplied. Flags declared after the first
 * prompted flag may not have been validated yet when the introduction runs.
 *
 * ```ts import.meta.vitest
 * import { Console, Effect } from "effect"
 * import { Command, Flag, Prompt } from "effect/cli"
 *
 * const program = Effect.gen(function*() {
 *   const intro = yield* Effect.cached(Console.log("Welcome to demo"))
 *
 *   const ask = (name: string) =>
 *     Flag.String(name).pipe(
 *       Flag.withFallbackPrompt(
 *         Effect.as(intro, Prompt.String({ message: `${name}?` }))
 *       )
 *     )
 *
 *   const demo = Command.make(
 *     "demo",
 *     { name: ask("name"), dir: ask("dir") },
 *     (input) => Console.log(input)
 *   ).pipe(Command.provideEffectDiscard(intro))
 *
 *   yield* Command.run(demo, { version: "1.0.0" })
 * })
 * ```
 *
 * @stability unstable
 * @category combinators
 * @since 4.0.0
 */
export declare const withFallbackPrompt: {
    /**
     * Adds a fallback prompt that is shown when a required flag is missing.
     *
     * **Gotchas**
     *
     * When `TerminalCapabilities.canPrompt` is `false`, the prompt is skipped and
     * the original `MissingOption` error is returned.
     *
     * **Example** (Falling back to prompts)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Flag, Prompt } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * const name = Flag.String("name").pipe(
     *   Flag.withFallbackPrompt(Prompt.String({ message: "Name" }))
     * )
     * name.kind // => "flag"
     * ```
     *
     * **Example** (Showing an introduction before fallback prompts)
     *
     * The prompt can be built by an effect, which only runs when the prompt is
     * needed. Share a cached introduction between the prompt builders and
     * `Command.provideEffectDiscard`: it runs once, before the first prompt, and
     * also runs when every flag is supplied. Flags declared after the first
     * prompted flag may not have been validated yet when the introduction runs.
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Console, Effect } from "effect"
     * import { Command, Flag, Prompt } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * const program = Effect.gen(function*() {
     *   const intro = yield* Effect.cached(Console.log("Welcome to demo"))
     *
     *   const ask = (name: string) =>
     *     Flag.String(name).pipe(
     *       Flag.withFallbackPrompt(
     *         Effect.as(intro, Prompt.String({ message: `${name}?` }))
     *       )
     *     )
     *
     *   const demo = Command.make(
     *     "demo",
     *     { name: ask("name"), dir: ask("dir") },
     *     (input) => Console.log(input)
     *   ).pipe(Command.provideEffectDiscard(intro))
     *
     *   yield* Command.run(demo, { version: "1.0.0" })
     * })
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category combinators
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <B>(prompt: Param.FallbackPrompt<B>): <A>(self: Flag<A>) => Flag<A | B>;
    /**
     * Adds a fallback prompt that is shown when a required flag is missing.
     *
     * **Gotchas**
     *
     * When `TerminalCapabilities.canPrompt` is `false`, the prompt is skipped and
     * the original `MissingOption` error is returned.
     *
     * **Example** (Falling back to prompts)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Flag, Prompt } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * const name = Flag.String("name").pipe(
     *   Flag.withFallbackPrompt(Prompt.String({ message: "Name" }))
     * )
     * name.kind // => "flag"
     * ```
     *
     * **Example** (Showing an introduction before fallback prompts)
     *
     * The prompt can be built by an effect, which only runs when the prompt is
     * needed. Share a cached introduction between the prompt builders and
     * `Command.provideEffectDiscard`: it runs once, before the first prompt, and
     * also runs when every flag is supplied. Flags declared after the first
     * prompted flag may not have been validated yet when the introduction runs.
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Console, Effect } from "effect"
     * import { Command, Flag, Prompt } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * const program = Effect.gen(function*() {
     *   const intro = yield* Effect.cached(Console.log("Welcome to demo"))
     *
     *   const ask = (name: string) =>
     *     Flag.String(name).pipe(
     *       Flag.withFallbackPrompt(
     *         Effect.as(intro, Prompt.String({ message: `${name}?` }))
     *       )
     *     )
     *
     *   const demo = Command.make(
     *     "demo",
     *     { name: ask("name"), dir: ask("dir") },
     *     (input) => Console.log(input)
     *   ).pipe(Command.provideEffectDiscard(intro))
     *
     *   yield* Command.run(demo, { version: "1.0.0" })
     * })
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category combinators
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A, B>(self: Flag<A>, prompt: Param.FallbackPrompt<B>): Flag<A | B>;
};
/**
 * Transforms the parsed value of a flag using a mapping function.
 *
 * **Example** (Mapping parsed values)
 *
 * ```ts import.meta.vitest
 * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
 *
 * // Convert string to uppercase
 * const nameFlag = Flag.String("name").pipe(
 *   Flag.map((name) => name.toUpperCase())
 * )
 *
 * // Convert port to URL
 * const urlFlag = Flag.Int("port").pipe(
 *   Flag.map((port) => `http://localhost:${port}`)
 * )
 * const kinds = [nameFlag.kind, urlFlag.kind] // => ["flag", "flag"]
 * ```
 *
 * @stability unstable
 * @category mapping
 * @since 4.0.0
 */
export declare const map: {
    /**
     * Transforms the parsed value of a flag using a mapping function.
     *
     * **Example** (Mapping parsed values)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * // Convert string to uppercase
     * const nameFlag = Flag.String("name").pipe(
     *   Flag.map((name) => name.toUpperCase())
     * )
     *
     * // Convert port to URL
     * const urlFlag = Flag.Int("port").pipe(
     *   Flag.map((port) => `http://localhost:${port}`)
     * )
     * const kinds = [nameFlag.kind, urlFlag.kind] // => ["flag", "flag"]
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category mapping
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A, B>(f: (a: A) => B): (self: Flag<A>) => Flag<B>;
    /**
     * Transforms the parsed value of a flag using a mapping function.
     *
     * **Example** (Mapping parsed values)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * // Convert string to uppercase
     * const nameFlag = Flag.String("name").pipe(
     *   Flag.map((name) => name.toUpperCase())
     * )
     *
     * // Convert port to URL
     * const urlFlag = Flag.Int("port").pipe(
     *   Flag.map((port) => `http://localhost:${port}`)
     * )
     * const kinds = [nameFlag.kind, urlFlag.kind] // => ["flag", "flag"]
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category mapping
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A, B>(self: Flag<A>, f: (a: A) => B): Flag<B>;
};
/**
 * Transforms the parsed value using an Effect that can perform IO operations.
 *
 * **Example** (Mapping parsed values effectfully)
 *
 * ```ts import.meta.vitest
 * import { Effect, FileSystem, Layer, Path, Stdio, Terminal } from "effect"
 * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
 * import { ChildProcessSpawner } from "effect/process"
 *
 * const CliTestLayer = Layer.mergeAll(
 *   FileSystem.layerNoop({}),
 *   Path.layer,
 *   Stdio.layerTest({}),
 *   Layer.succeed(Terminal.Terminal, Terminal.make({
 *     columns: Effect.succeed(80),
 *     rows: Effect.succeed(24),
 *     size: Effect.die("unused"),
 *     readInput: Effect.die("unused"),
 *     readLine: Effect.die("unused"),
 *     display: () => Effect.void
 *   })),
 *   Layer.succeed(
 *     ChildProcessSpawner.ChildProcessSpawner,
 *     ChildProcessSpawner.make(() => Effect.die("unused"))
 *   )
 * )
 *
 * const upperName = Flag.String("name").pipe(
 *   Flag.mapEffect((name) => Effect.succeed(name.toUpperCase()))
 * )
 *
 * const [, value] = await Effect.runPromise(
 *   upperName.parse({
 *     arguments: [],
 *     flags: { name: ["alice"] }
 *   }).pipe(Effect.provide(CliTestLayer))
 * )
 * value // => "ALICE"
 * ```
 *
 * @stability unstable
 * @category mapping
 * @since 4.0.0
 */
export declare const mapEffect: {
    /**
     * Transforms the parsed value using an Effect that can perform IO operations.
     *
     * **Example** (Mapping parsed values effectfully)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Effect, FileSystem, Layer, Path, Stdio, Terminal } from "effect"
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     * import { ChildProcessSpawner } from "effect/process"
     *
     * const CliTestLayer = Layer.mergeAll(
     *   FileSystem.layerNoop({}),
     *   Path.layer,
     *   Stdio.layerTest({}),
     *   Layer.succeed(Terminal.Terminal, Terminal.make({
     *     columns: Effect.succeed(80),
     *     rows: Effect.succeed(24),
     *     size: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     readInput: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     readLine: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     display: () => Effect.void
     *   })),
     *   Layer.succeed(
     *     ChildProcessSpawner.ChildProcessSpawner,
     *     ChildProcessSpawner.make(() => Effect.die("unused"))
     *   )
     * )
     *
     * const upperName = Flag.String("name").pipe(
     *   Flag.mapEffect((name) => Effect.succeed(name.toUpperCase()))
     * )
     *
     * const [, value] = await Effect.runPromise(
     *   upperName.parse({
     *     arguments: [],
     *     flags: { name: ["alice"] }
     *   }).pipe(Effect.provide(CliTestLayer))
     * )
     * value // => "ALICE"
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category mapping
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A, B>(f: (a: A) => Effect.Effect<B, CliError.CliError, Environment>): (self: Flag<A>) => Flag<B>;
    /**
     * Transforms the parsed value using an Effect that can perform IO operations.
     *
     * **Example** (Mapping parsed values effectfully)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Effect, FileSystem, Layer, Path, Stdio, Terminal } from "effect"
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     * import { ChildProcessSpawner } from "effect/process"
     *
     * const CliTestLayer = Layer.mergeAll(
     *   FileSystem.layerNoop({}),
     *   Path.layer,
     *   Stdio.layerTest({}),
     *   Layer.succeed(Terminal.Terminal, Terminal.make({
     *     columns: Effect.succeed(80),
     *     rows: Effect.succeed(24),
     *     size: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     readInput: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     readLine: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     display: () => Effect.void
     *   })),
     *   Layer.succeed(
     *     ChildProcessSpawner.ChildProcessSpawner,
     *     ChildProcessSpawner.make(() => Effect.die("unused"))
     *   )
     * )
     *
     * const upperName = Flag.String("name").pipe(
     *   Flag.mapEffect((name) => Effect.succeed(name.toUpperCase()))
     * )
     *
     * const [, value] = await Effect.runPromise(
     *   upperName.parse({
     *     arguments: [],
     *     flags: { name: ["alice"] }
     *   }).pipe(Effect.provide(CliTestLayer))
     * )
     * value // => "ALICE"
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category mapping
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A, B>(self: Flag<A>, f: (a: A) => Effect.Effect<B, CliError.CliError, Environment>): Flag<B>;
};
/**
 * Transforms the parsed value using a function that might throw, with error handling.
 *
 * **Example** (Mapping thrown errors)
 *
 * ```ts import.meta.vitest
 * import { Effect, FileSystem, Layer, Path, Stdio, Terminal } from "effect"
 * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
 * import { ChildProcessSpawner } from "effect/process"
 *
 * const CliTestLayer = Layer.mergeAll(
 *   FileSystem.layerNoop({}),
 *   Path.layer,
 *   Stdio.layerTest({}),
 *   Layer.succeed(Terminal.Terminal, Terminal.make({
 *     columns: Effect.succeed(80),
 *     rows: Effect.succeed(24),
 *     size: Effect.die("unused"),
 *     readInput: Effect.die("unused"),
 *     readLine: Effect.die("unused"),
 *     display: () => Effect.void
 *   })),
 *   Layer.succeed(
 *     ChildProcessSpawner.ChildProcessSpawner,
 *     ChildProcessSpawner.make(() => Effect.die("unused"))
 *   )
 * )
 *
 * // Parse JSON string with error handling
 * const jsonFlag = Flag.String("config").pipe(
 *   Flag.mapTryCatch(
 *     (json) => JSON.parse(json),
 *     (error) => `Invalid JSON: ${error}`
 *   )
 * )
 *
 * // Parse URL with error handling
 * const urlFlag = Flag.String("url").pipe(
 *   Flag.mapTryCatch(
 *     (url) => new URL(url),
 *     (error) => `Invalid URL: ${error}`
 *   )
 * )
 *
 * const [, value] = await Effect.runPromise(
 *   jsonFlag.parse({
 *     arguments: [],
 *     flags: { config: ['{"enabled":true}'] }
 *   }).pipe(Effect.provide(CliTestLayer))
 * )
 * value // => { enabled: true }
 * ```
 *
 * @stability unstable
 * @category mapping
 * @since 4.0.0
 */
export declare const mapTryCatch: {
    /**
     * Transforms the parsed value using a function that might throw, with error handling.
     *
     * **Example** (Mapping thrown errors)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Effect, FileSystem, Layer, Path, Stdio, Terminal } from "effect"
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     * import { ChildProcessSpawner } from "effect/process"
     *
     * const CliTestLayer = Layer.mergeAll(
     *   FileSystem.layerNoop({}),
     *   Path.layer,
     *   Stdio.layerTest({}),
     *   Layer.succeed(Terminal.Terminal, Terminal.make({
     *     columns: Effect.succeed(80),
     *     rows: Effect.succeed(24),
     *     size: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     readInput: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     readLine: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     display: () => Effect.void
     *   })),
     *   Layer.succeed(
     *     ChildProcessSpawner.ChildProcessSpawner,
     *     ChildProcessSpawner.make(() => Effect.die("unused"))
     *   )
     * )
     *
     * // Parse JSON string with error handling
     * const jsonFlag = Flag.String("config").pipe(
     *   Flag.mapTryCatch(
     *     (json) => JSON.parse(json),
     *     (error) => `Invalid JSON: ${error}`
     *   )
     * )
     *
     * // Parse URL with error handling
     * const urlFlag = Flag.String("url").pipe(
     *   Flag.mapTryCatch(
     *     (url) => new URL(url),
     *     (error) => `Invalid URL: ${error}`
     *   )
     * )
     *
     * const [, value] = await Effect.runPromise(
     *   jsonFlag.parse({
     *     arguments: [],
     *     flags: { config: ['{"enabled":true}'] }
     *   }).pipe(Effect.provide(CliTestLayer))
     * )
     * value // => { enabled: true }
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category mapping
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A, B>(f: (a: A) => B, onError: (error: unknown) => string): (self: Flag<A>) => Flag<B>;
    /**
     * Transforms the parsed value using a function that might throw, with error handling.
     *
     * **Example** (Mapping thrown errors)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Effect, FileSystem, Layer, Path, Stdio, Terminal } from "effect"
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     * import { ChildProcessSpawner } from "effect/process"
     *
     * const CliTestLayer = Layer.mergeAll(
     *   FileSystem.layerNoop({}),
     *   Path.layer,
     *   Stdio.layerTest({}),
     *   Layer.succeed(Terminal.Terminal, Terminal.make({
     *     columns: Effect.succeed(80),
     *     rows: Effect.succeed(24),
     *     size: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     readInput: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     readLine: Effect.die("unused"),
     *     display: () => Effect.void
     *   })),
     *   Layer.succeed(
     *     ChildProcessSpawner.ChildProcessSpawner,
     *     ChildProcessSpawner.make(() => Effect.die("unused"))
     *   )
     * )
     *
     * // Parse JSON string with error handling
     * const jsonFlag = Flag.String("config").pipe(
     *   Flag.mapTryCatch(
     *     (json) => JSON.parse(json),
     *     (error) => `Invalid JSON: ${error}`
     *   )
     * )
     *
     * // Parse URL with error handling
     * const urlFlag = Flag.String("url").pipe(
     *   Flag.mapTryCatch(
     *     (url) => new URL(url),
     *     (error) => `Invalid URL: ${error}`
     *   )
     * )
     *
     * const [, value] = await Effect.runPromise(
     *   jsonFlag.parse({
     *     arguments: [],
     *     flags: { config: ['{"enabled":true}'] }
     *   }).pipe(Effect.provide(CliTestLayer))
     * )
     * value // => { enabled: true }
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category mapping
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A, B>(self: Flag<A>, f: (a: A) => B, onError: (error: unknown) => string): Flag<B>;
};
/**
 * Ensures a flag is specified at least a minimum number of times.
 *
 * **Example** (Requiring repeated values)
 *
 * ```ts import.meta.vitest
 * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
 *
 * const sourceFlag = Flag.atLeast(Flag.File("source"), 2)
 * // Requires at least 2 source files
 * // Usage: --source file1.ts --source file2.ts
 *
 * const tagFlag = Flag.String("tag").pipe(
 *   Flag.atLeast(1)
 * )
 * // Requires at least 1 tag
 * const kinds = [sourceFlag.kind, tagFlag.kind] // => ["flag", "flag"]
 * ```
 *
 * @stability unstable
 * @category repetition
 * @since 4.0.0
 */
export declare const atLeast: {
    /**
     * Ensures a flag is specified at least a minimum number of times.
     *
     * **Example** (Requiring repeated values)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * const sourceFlag = Flag.atLeast(Flag.File("source"), 2)
     * // Requires at least 2 source files
     * // Usage: --source file1.ts --source file2.ts
     *
     * const tagFlag = Flag.String("tag").pipe(
     *   Flag.atLeast(1)
     * )
     * // Requires at least 1 tag
     * const kinds = [sourceFlag.kind, tagFlag.kind] // => ["flag", "flag"]
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category repetition
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A>(min: number): (self: Flag<A>) => Flag<ReadonlyArray<A>>;
    /**
     * Ensures a flag is specified at least a minimum number of times.
     *
     * **Example** (Requiring repeated values)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"
     *
     * const sourceFlag = Flag.atLeast(Flag.File("source"), 2)
     * // Requires at least 2 source files
     * // Usage: --source file1.ts --source file2.ts
     *
     * const tagFlag = Flag.String("tag").pipe(
     *   Flag.atLeast(1)
     * )
     * // Requires at least 1 tag
     * const kinds = [sourceFlag.kind, tagFlag.kind] // => ["flag", "flag"]
     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category repetition
     * @since 4.0.0
     */
    <A>(self: Flag<A>, min: number): Flag<ReadonlyArray<A>>;
};
/**
 * Ensures a flag is specified at most a maximum number of times.
 *
 * **Example** (Limiting repeated values)
 *
 * ```ts import.meta.vitest
 * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"

 * // Usage: --warning w1 --warning w2 --warning w3
 *
 * const debugFlag = Flag.String("debug").pipe(
 *   Flag.atMost(1)
 * )
 * // Allows at most 1 debug flag
 * const kinds = [warningFlag.kind, debugFlag.kind] // => ["flag", "flag"]
 * ```
 *
 * @stability unstable
 * @category repetition
 * @since 4.0.0
 */
export declare const atMost: {
    /**

     * **Example** (Limiting repeated values)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest

     *   Flag.atMost(1)
     * )
     * // Allows at most 1 debug flag

     * ```
     *
     * @stability unstable
     * @category repetition
     * @since 4.0.0

    <A>(max: number): (self: Flag<A>) => Flag<ReadonlyArray<A>>;
    /**

     * **Example** (Limiting repeated values)
     *
     * ```ts import.meta.vitest
     * import { Flag } from "effect/cli"

    <A>(self: Flag<A>, max: number): Flag<ReadonlyArray<A>>;
